Preparation (1-4 points)\n   - 1 point: The group has not adequately prepared, with many lines missed or read incorrectly. Props and costumes, if used, are not well-thought-out or do not enhance the performance.\n   - 2 points: The group has prepared somewhat, but there are still some lines missed or read incorrectly. Props and costumes, if used, are basic and could be improved.\n   - 3 points: The group has prepared well, with only a few minor mistakes in lines. Props and costumes, if used, are appropriate and add to the performance.\n   - 4 points: The group has thoroughly prepared, with no mistakes in lines. Props and costumes, if used, are creative, well-crafted, and significantly enhance the performance.\n\n2. Expression and Engagement (1-4 points)\n   - 1 point: The readers show little expression or engagement, making it difficult for the audience to follow the story or connect with the characters.\n   - 2 points: The readers show some expression and engagement, but it could be more consistent and varied throughout the performance.\n   - 3 points: The readers demonstrate good expression and engagement, using vocal variety, facial expressions, and body language to bring the characters and story to life.\n   - 4 points: The readers exhibit excellent expression and engagement, fully embodying their characters and captivating the audience with their performances.\n\n3. Fluency and Pacing (1-4 points)\n   - 1 point: The readers struggle with fluency, often stumbling over words or reading at an inappropriate pace, which disrupts the flow of the performance.\n   - 2 points: The readers demonstrate some fluency, but there are still moments of hesitation or uneven pacing that could be improved.\n   - 3 points: The readers show good fluency and pacing, reading smoothly and at an appropriate speed that allows the audience to follow the story easily.\n   - 4 points: The readers exhibit excellent fluency and pacing, reading with confidence and control, and adjusting their speed to enhance the dramatic impact of the performance.\n\n4.
